Ablations indicate that the agro-environmental label and spatial instance expansion (multiple grid-node weather sequences per municipality-year) contribute positively without increasing input complexity. SHAP diagnostics suggest crop year explains most of the long-run trajectory, whereas within-season weather and agro-environmental context primarily drive interannual deviations, with moisture/cloud and thermal-demand variables dominating. Overall, the framework is straightforward to deploy across other crops and geographic regions and is naturally compatible with operational weather forecasts for routine monitoring.

</details>


### [173] [Grokking through the Lens of Minimum-Norm Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38453)
*Gil Kur,Ileana Rugina,Clémentine Carla Juliette Dominé,Marco Mondelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文建立统计理论，刻画正则化几何与信号稀疏性如何在插值附近支配泛化，揭示稀疏正则化下精确插值优于近似拟合的机制，并证明零一泛化律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Grokking现象表明拟合训练数据与学到真实信号发生在不同阶段，但现有理论对"延迟泛化"如何依赖归纳偏置和信号结构缺乏定量解释。作者旨在填补这一空白，重点研究插值附近的泛化行为。

Method: 以高维回归为原型设定，发展统计理论：分析正则化几何（范数形状）与信号稀疏性如何影响泛化。在强过参数化无噪声问题中证明零一泛化律，构造一族凸范数，其插值器在训练误差为0的情况下从全零预测器的平凡风险过渡到精确恢复；在特征维度与样本量成比例时，精确刻画ℓ_r正则化路径上的训练与泛化误差。另在对角线性网络和模算术Transformer上做实验验证。

Result: 证明零一泛化律并构造过渡范数族；给出ℓ_r正则化路径误差的精确刻画；量化近插值处的泛化增益——该增益随范数更稀疏促进、目标更稀疏而增大，在无噪声与ℓ_1正则化时出现急剧下降；实验（对角线性网络、模算术Transformer）验证理论预测。

Conclusion: 稀疏促进正则可使精确插值比近似拟合更准确；除grokking外，最小范数插值存在统计不稳定性：正则化强度的微小扰动即可导致泛化性能剧变，同时训练误差仍保持很小。

Abstract: Grokking shows that fitting the training data and learning the underlying signal can occur at very different stages. However, existing theories offer limited quantitative insight into how this delayed generalization depends on inductive bias and signal structure. Our work addresses the gap by developing a statistical theory that characterizes how regularization geometry and signal sparsity govern generalization near interpolation. In particular, we focus on the prototypical setting of high-dimensional regression and identify regimes in which sparsity-promoting regularization makes exact interpolation much more accurate than approximate fitting. In strongly overparameterized noiseless problems, we prove a zero--one generalization law and construct a family of convex norms whose interpolators transition from the trivial risk of the all-zero predictor to exact recovery, while keeping the training error equal to $0$. Furthermore, when feature dimension and sample size are proportional, we provide a precise characterization of training and generalization errors along $\ell_r$-regularization paths. This in turn allows us to quantify the generalization gain that remains near interpolation: we show that this gain increases as the norm becomes more sparsity-promoting and as the target becomes sparser, with a sharp drop in generalization reached for noiseless data and $\ell_1$ regularization. Experiments on diagonal linear networks and transformers trained on modular arithmetic demonstrate the generality of our theoretical predictions. Finally, beyond grokking, our work reveals a statistical instability in minimum-norm interpolation: small perturbations in the regularization strength can lead to drastically different generalization, while preserving small training error.

</details>


### [174] [Does Gradient Conflict Predict the Understanding--Generation Trade-off? A Controlled Audit of Conflict-Metric Validity in Unified Multimodal Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38465)
*Shuyang Jiang,Fucheng Deng,Yuchuan Luo,Zhenyu Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文对"梯度冲突指标可预测多模态理解-生成权衡"这一前提进行了直接审计，发现方向性冲突指标与最终权衡无可靠相关性，且抑制冲突不改变权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多模态模型（UMMs）的设计越来越多地围绕理解与生成目标之间的梯度冲突展开，但"降低这些冲突指标即可改善下游理解-生成权衡"这一前提从未被直接检验。作者旨在建立可验证的审计标准。

Method: 构建受控测试平台 GRIDUMM，复现 UMM 训练的关键结构性要素，同时使真实的权衡可精确计算。进行 63 种配置、372 个检查点的测量，并对冲突进行单调抑制的剂量-反应干预实验。

Result: 没有任何方向性冲突指标与最终权衡的绝对 Spearman 相关性达到 0.3 且置信区间排除零；单调抑制冲突的干预使权衡保持平稳，分离了相关性与因果性。范数比是生成失败的检测器，但在已掌握生成的配置中失效。功能干扰度量优于方向性冲突指标，而训练损失对权衡有很强的跟踪能力。

Conclusion: 研究并非表明冲突无用，而是说明其作为诊断目标的有效性必须被实证确立而非默认假设。作者发布审计协议作为可复用的标准。

Abstract: Unified multimodal models (UMMs) are increasingly designed around gradient conflict between understanding and generation objectives. The premise that reducing these metrics improves the downstream understanding-generation trade-off has never been tested directly. We audit it in a controlled testbed, GRIDUMM, which mirrors key structural ingredients of UMM training while making the ground-truth trade-off exactly computable. Across 63 configurations and 372 measured checkpoints, no directional conflict metric reaches an absolute Spearman correlation of 0.3 with a confidence interval excluding zero for conflict measured during training against the eventual trade-off. A dose-response intervention that monotonically suppresses conflict leaves the trade-off flat, separating correlation from causation. The norm ratio is a generation-failure detector and becomes null among configurations that master generation. Functional interference measures outperform directional conflict metrics, while training loss tracks the trade-off strongly. Our results do not show that conflict is useless; they show that its validity as a diagnostic target must be established, not assumed, and we release the audit protocol as a reusable standard.

</details>


### [175] [RetroGEF: Dynamic Graph Edit Flow for Single-Step Retrosynthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38484)
*Xiaozhuang Song,Xuemin Chen,Xinjian Zhao,Yaoyao Xu,Tianshu Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RetroGEF：基于流的单步逆合成生成模型，在同一生成过程中建模分子图变换与图尺寸变化，无需固定尺寸图画布和预设编辑顺序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逆合成在现代药物发现与材料设计中处于核心地位。该任务涉及可同时改变连接关系与图尺寸的分子图变换，并可能引入目标分子中不存在的反应物组分，同时修改源自产物的结构。已有方法通常依赖固定尺寸的图画布或预设编辑顺序，难以自然刻画上述变换。

Method: 提出 RetroGEF，一种用于单步逆合成的流（flow-based）生成模型。以目标分子为起点，通过在分子图中添加原子和改变化学键来构造可能的反应物；在同一生成过程中同时建模分子变换与图尺寸变化，而非依赖固定尺寸图；直接从产物–反应物配对中学习该过程，无需预先规定的编辑顺序。

Result: 在具有代表性的逆合成基准上，RetroGEF 取得了当前最优（state-of-the-art）性能。

Conclusion: RetroGEF 通过流式生成统一建模分子图变换与图尺寸变化，并免去编辑顺序约束，为单步逆合成提供了有效的新范式。

Abstract: Retrosynthesis enables the discovery of viable synthetic routes to target molecules. It plays a central role in modern drug discovery and materials design. Retrosynthesis involves molecular graph transformations that can change both connectivity and graph size. These transformations may introduce reactant components absent from the target while revising the product-derived structure. To model these transformations, we propose RetroGEF, a flow-based generative model for single-step retrosynthesis. Starting from the target molecule, it constructs possible reactants by adding atoms and changing bonds in the molecular graph. RetroGEF models molecular transformations and changes in graph size within the same generative process, rather than relying on a fixed-size graph canvas. It learns this process directly from product--reactant pairs without requiring a prescribed edit order. Experiments on representative retrosynthesis benchmarks demonstrate that RetroGEF achieves state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [176] [Role-guided Speaker Deletion Verification in Clinical Psychiatry Speech Recordings with Audio Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38491)
*Joseph T Colonel,Daniel Katzman,Kelsey Kirker,Adam N Davidson,Shalaila S Haas,Cheryl Corcoran,René S Kahn,Guillermo Checci,Baihan Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究针对精神科双人临床对话录音中"指定说话人（临床医生或患者）语音是否已被彻底移除"的验证问题，提出两条对称流水线：先对目标角色语音进行红action（消音/删除），再用音频-语言模型与大语言模型扫描剩余音频以识别遗漏的删除。在48段双人录音语料上，对四个开放权重模型（Gemma-4-12B、Gemma-4-31B、Nemotron-3-Nano、Nemotron-3-Nano-Omni）进行仅推理评估，14种模型-视图配置的析取OR集成取得综合F1为0.478（精确率0.330，召回率0.870），优于单个模型，召回增益表明模型与上下文视图之间存在显著互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精神科临床研究日益依赖大规模语音数据采集以识别声学与语言学生物标记，但不断演变的知情同意与协议要求，迫使研究者在多说话人录音中移除指定说话人（如临床医生或患者）的语音，并在整个语料库规模上验证移除是否彻底——该规模下人工逐条复核不可行。因此需要自动化的验证方法。

Method: 以角色驱动的双人精神科临床对话为对象，构建两条平行且对称的流水线：一条确认临床医生语音已从访谈录音中移除，另一条确认患者语音已从同一批录音中移除。每条流水线先对其目标角色的原始音频进行红action处理，随后用音频-语言模型和大语言模型扫描剩余输出，识别被遗漏的删除。在48段双人录音语料上进行评估，采用仅推理设置测试四个开放权重模型（Gemma-4-12B、Gemma-4-31B、Nemotron-3-Nano、Nemotron-3-Nano-Omni），并考察14种模型-视图配置的析取OR集成。

Result: 在48段双人录音语料上，14种模型-视图配置的析取OR集成综合F1为0.478（精确率0.330，召回率0.870），相较单个模型估计有所提升；提升主要来自召回增益，表明不同模型与上下文视图之间存在显著的互补性。

Conclusion: 基于音频-语言模型与大语言模型的自动扫描可在大规模语料上验证指定说话人语音是否被彻底移除，析取OR集成通过模型与上下文视图的互补性显著提升召回，为该类合规验证提供了可行路径；但精确率仍偏低（0.330），是后续改进的重点方向。

Abstract: Clinical research in psychiatry increasingly relies on large scale collection of spoken language data to identify acoustic and linguistic biomarkers. Yet evolving consent and protocol requirements can oblige investigators to remove a designated speaker from multi-speaker recordings and to verify said removal at a scale infeasible for manual review of entire corpora. We study this verification problem for role-driven dyadic clinical dialogue in psychiatry and investigate it with two parallel, symmetric pipelines: confirming that clinician speech has been removed from psychiatric interview recordings, and confirming that patient speech has been removed from the same recordings. Each pipeline redacts the raw audio for its target role and then scans the surviving output with audio-language and large-language models to identify missed deletions. We evaluate this approach on a corpus of 48 dyadic recordings drawn from psychiatry settings, testing four open-weight models in an inference-only setting: Gemma-4-12B, Gemma-4-31B, Nemotron-3-Nano, and Nemotron-3-Nano-Omni. A disjunctive OR ensemble over fourteen model-view configurations had a combined F1 of 0.478 (precision 0.330, recall 0.870), an improvement over individual model estimates driven by recall gains that point to substantial complementarity across models and context views.

</details>


### [177] [Autoregressive Frontier Expansion: Growing Trees with Graph Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38506)
*Umer Gupta,Saku Peltonen,Martin Ritzert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Autoregressive Frontier Expansion，一种通过迭代扩张过程生成树状结构的生成框架，利用 SO(2)-等变 GNN 参数化的流匹配模型预测前沿分支的分叉或终止，在皮层神经元和植物树的生成任务中取得与参考分布高度一致的形态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 树状分支结构（植物树、神经元、血管、呼吸道树）普遍存在，其分支形态往往反映功能，因此结构建模对理解这些系统至关重要。然而获取真实世界 3D 数据往往昂贵甚至不可行，因此需要逼真的生成模型用于模拟和数据增强。现有方法存在问题：针对特定形态的模型要么限制拓扑的生成方式，要么依赖手工调参的机制性流程；而通用 3D 图生成器则不利用也不约束树的结构特征。因此，作者希望构建一个既通用又能显式利用树结构、并模拟真实树木生物学生长的生成框架。

Method: 提出 Autoregressive Frontier Expansion 生成框架，通过迭代扩张过程构建树，模拟真实树木的生物学生长。每一步中，由 SO(2)-等变 GNN 参数化的流匹配（flow-matching）模型扩张前沿，预测每个活跃分支是分叉还是终止。在皮层神经元和植物树上进行评估，涵盖无条件生成、类别条件生成和形态引导生成三类任务。

Result: 在两个领域（皮层神经元与植物树）上，生成的形态与参考分布高度接近；在条件生成实验中，生成结果也与指定目标高度吻合。

Conclusion: Autoregressive Frontier Expansion 通过模拟生物学生长的自回归前沿扩张机制，结合 SO(2)-等变 GNN 与流匹配，为树状结构提供了一种既能利用树结构先验又具备通用性的生成建模方法，在无条件、类别条件和形态引导生成任务中均表现出与参考分布和目标一致的良好性能。

Abstract: Tree-like branching structures are common in nature, from botanical trees to neurons, blood vessels and respiratory trees. Their branching shape often reflects function, making structural modelling central to understanding how these systems work. Because acquiring real-world 3D data is often expensive or infeasible, realistic generative models are valuable for simulation and data augmentation. Existing morphology-specific models either constrain how topology is generated or rely on hand-tuned, mechanistic procedures. Generic 3D graph generators, by contrast, do not exploit or enforce the structure of trees. We propose Autoregressive Frontier Expansion, a generative framework that constructs trees through an iterative expansion process, simulating the biological growth of real trees. At each step, a flow-matching model parameterised by an SO(2)-equivariant GNN expands the frontier by predicting whether each active branch bifurcates or terminates. We evaluate our method on cortical neurons and botanical trees in unconditional, class-conditioned, and morphology-guided generation. Across both domains, the generated morphologies agree closely with the reference distributions and, in conditional experiments, with the specified targets.

</details>


### [178] [Does Text Steer Neural PDE Surrogates? A Controlled Diagnostic with OperatorCLIP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38517)
*Aadi Dash,Lennon J. Shikhman,Michael Galarnyk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过OperatorCLIP框架研究文本条件神经代理模型的"归因问题"：仅凭文本条件模型误差更低，并不能证明模型真正利用了文本语义。作者比较了无条件FNO、常量句子FiLM控制和任务描述对比对齐三种设置，在三个数据集上发现常量条件在2D任务上误差反而更低，且文本编码器无法可靠识别匹配对，提示需要路径对照实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本条件神经代理模型常通过更低误差被解读为"利用了文本语义"，但误差降低本身并不能证明模型真正使用了文本含义。作者希望厘清这一归因问题（attribution problem），区分"文本条件带来的表面性能提升"与"模型真正理解文本语义"两件不同的事。

Method: 提出OperatorCLIP框架，设置三组对照：(1) 无条件FNO；(2) 常量句子FiLM控制；(3) 固定任务描述经对比对齐训练。在Darcy2D、ShallowWater2D和三维可压缩Navier-Stokes (CNS3D)上进行三随机种子实验，并做提示干预(prompt interventions)检验语义排序。

Result: 常量条件在两个2D任务上平均测试误差更低。相对该控制组，任务文本+对齐在ShallowWater2D和CNS3D上均值相近，在Darcy2D上均值更高；这些描述性比较存在较大种子不确定性。该对比同时改变了提示内容与损失函数，因此无法单独分离任一效应。文本编码器为从零训练，每个条件模型训练时仅见一个描述，此时成对InfoNCE无法识别匹配对且损失下界为log B。提示干预未显示可靠语义排序。

Conclusion: 这是一项方法论警示：需要路径对照(pathway controls)来验证文本条件模型是否真正利用语义。研究既未证明编码器具备语义能力，也未检验文本在不同物理语境下的有效性，仅说明低误差不能作为语义使用的证据。

Abstract: Lower error from a text-conditioned neural surrogate does not, by itself, show that the model uses the meaning of the text. We examine this attribution problem with OperatorCLIP, comparing an unconditioned FNO, a constant-sentence FiLM control, and a fixed task description trained with contrastive alignment. Three-seed experiments cover Darcy2D, ShallowWater2D, and three-dimensional compressible Navier-Stokes (CNS3D). Constant conditioning has lower mean test error on both 2D tasks. Relative to this control, task text plus alignment has a similar mean on ShallowWater2D and CNS3D and a higher mean on Darcy2D; these descriptive comparisons have substantial seed uncertainty. The latter comparison changes both prompt content and loss, so it isolates neither effect. The text encoder is trained from scratch, and each conditioned model sees only one description during training. In this regime, pairwise InfoNCE cannot identify matched pairs and has minimum $\log B$. Prompt interventions show no reliable semantic ordering. This methodological caution demonstrates why pathway controls are needed; it neither establishes semantic competence of the encoder nor tests the effectiveness of text under varying physical context.

</details>


### [179] [ShamAN-Q: Shampoo Augmented NanoQuant for Sub-1-bit LLM Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38521)
*Jonathan Mei,Sang Hyub Kim,Oliver Knitter,Chi Chen,Martin Roetteler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ShamAN-Q：一种亚1比特训练后量化方法，通过引入稠密曲率度量改进NanoQuant，在Qwen3-Base上显著降低困惑度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NanoQuant以对角重构几何进行亚1比特量化，但其对角近似忽略了权重间相关性，导致重构损失不够精确。作者希望用更稠密、更准确的曲率度量（借鉴Shampoo优化器的Kronecker分解思想）来提升量化效果。

Method: 用可处理的稠密曲率度量替换NanoQuant的对角重构几何。对每个线性权重，通过KL最小化将Kronecker积拟合到校准集的经验Fisher信息矩阵，由此构建Mahalanobis重构损失。连续ADMM更新转化为Sylvester方程求解，离散投影与部署格式保持不变。由于曲率依赖于具体权重，每层在因式分解前重新测量输入曲率统计，并周期性在部分量化的模型上刷新所有统计。此外还在相同总比特数下跨层重新分配NanoQuant的均匀秩。

Result: 在Qwen3-Base上，≈1 bpw时WikiText-2困惑度从27.56降至22.96（0.6B）、19.21降至16.72（1.7B）、14.29降至13.80（4B），同时在Eleuther LM Evaluation Harness上的零样本准确率持平或提升。在0.6B上，≈0.8 bpw即达到NanoQuant在≈1.0 bpw时公布的困惑度。

Conclusion: 将稠密Kronecker曲率度量引入亚1比特训练后量化，能在保持部署格式不变的前提下显著提升量化质量与比特效率。

Abstract: We introduce ShamAN-Q, a sub-1-bit post-training quantization method that extends NanoQuant by replacing each its diagonal reconstruction geometry with a tractable dense curvature metric, using a general paradigm popularized by the Shampoo optimizer. For each linear weight, ShamAN-Q fits a Kronecker product to the empirical Fisher information matrix of a small calibration set by Kullback--Leibler minimization, forming a Mahalanobis reconstruction loss from the result. The continuous ADMM updates from NanoQuant become solutions to Sylvester equations, while its discrete projection and deployment format remain unchanged. Because the curvature is local to a given set of weights, ShamAN-Q re-measures the input curvature statistic for each layer immediately before layer factorization, periodically refreshing all statistics on the partially quantized model. ShamAN-Q also redistributes the uniform rank from NanoQuant across layers at the same total number of bits. On Qwen3-Base, ShamAN-Q lowers WikiText-2 perplexity at $\approx$1 bpw from 27.56 to 22.96 (0.6B), 19.21 to 16.72 (1.7B), and 14.29 to 13.80 (4B) while matching or improving zero-shot accuracy on the Eleuther LM Evaluation Harness. On 0.6B, ShamAN-Q at $\approx$0.8 bpw matches the published perplexity of NanoQuant at $\approx$1.0 bpw.

</details>


### [180] [MM-FinEval: A Multi-Task Multimodal Benchmark for Real-World Financial Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38523)
*Dong Shu,Yanguang Liu,Huopu Zhang,Saisai Hu,Haiyan Zhao,Hekun Huang,Mengnan Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出MM-FinEval，一个面向财报电话会议的多模态、多任务基准，含2,045个S&P 500电话会议样本与12类金融任务标签，覆盖文本、幻灯片图像与音频三种模态，并评测19个基线模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融基准多局限于单模态输入或单任务设定，难以评估多模态大语言模型能否支撑真实金融分析，尤其是财报电话会议中复杂的公司披露、市场预期与沟通信号推理需求。

Method: 构建MM-FinEval基准，时间跨度为2019至2022年，包含2,045个S&P 500财报电话会议作为输入、12个金融任务标签作为输出；每个输入含词级文本转录、对应演示幻灯片、完整音频录音三种模态。评测19个基线模型，分为Image-Text、Audio-Text、Any-to-Any三类配置。

Result: 处理三种模态的小规模Any-to-Any模型表现强劲，甚至优于仅支持两模态输入的更大规模专有模型，说明三模态设计引入了有用且非冗余的信息。

Conclusion: 文本、音频与视觉数据构成重要且互补的信号，模拟了专家人类分析师决策过程，验证了多模态多任务金融基准的必要性与有效性。

Abstract: Financial forecasting from earnings conference calls requires models to reason over complex corporate disclosures, market expectations, and subtle communication signals. However, existing financial benchmarks are often limited to unimodal inputs or single-task settings, making it difficult to evaluate whether multimodal large language models (LLMs) can support real-world financial analysis. In this paper, we introduce MM-FinEval, a novel benchmark designed to evaluate multimodal LLMs across multiple financial tasks. MM-FinEval spans a diverse timeline from 2019 to 2022. The entire proposed dataset contains 2,045 S\&P 500 conference earning calls as inputs and 12 financial task labels as outputs. Each input contains three modalities: a word-to-word text transcript of the earning call, the corresponding presentation slides used during the call, and the entire audio recording. To establish a rigorous evaluation framework, we analyze 19 baseline models across three distinct model categories: Image-Text, Audio-Text, and Any-to-Any configurations. We observe that small-size Any-to-Any models processing all three modalities achieve strong performance, even when compared against larger proprietary models restricted to two-modality inputs. This indicates that our tri-modal dataset design introduces useful, non-redundant information. These results validate that text, audio, and visual data serve as important, complementary signals that mimic the decision-making process of expert human analysts.

</details>


### [181] [Revisiting scaling laws for reward optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38526)
*Ali Aouad,Aymane El Gadarri,Vivek F. Farias*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一个联合刻画奖励模型训练数据量M与策略KL散度预算K的性能缩放律：性能约为Θ(√min{log M, K})，通过信息论模型给出上界并证明紧致可达，实证拟合R²达97%–99%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有关于AI对齐中针对奖励模型优化的缩放律只刻画了性能与优化强度（以相对参考策略的KL散度预算衡量）的关系，而代理奖励模型的准确度又取决于训练所用的偏好数据量，但此前研究未能清晰识别性能如何同时随训练数据量与散度预算共同缩放。

Method: 建立信息论模型推导性能上界，并通过构造性过程证明该上界可紧致达成；在真实标注场景下开展大量实证评估：用70B的gold奖励模型生成反馈数据，再用能力较弱的模型（0.6B–4B）训练代理奖励模型，涵盖不同模型规模、噪声水平及优化方式（best-of-N与policy tilting）。

Result: 性能大致按Θ(√min{log M, K})缩放；该缩放律拟合极佳（R²为97%至99%），优于其他替代设定，并在不同模型规模、噪声和优化流程下保持稳健。证据表明奖励优化类似于一个简单的选择任务：利用带噪声的偏好反馈从一系列IID高斯随机变量中做选择。

Conclusion: 奖励优化在数据量与散度预算的联合作用下遵循简洁的缩放律，且可用IID高斯选择任务作类比；该理论框架与实证结果一致，为理解过优化与奖励黑客现象提供了新的定量视角。

Abstract: Scaling laws for optimization against reward models in AI alignment have pinned down how performance depends on optimization effort---measured by a KL-divergence budget relative to a reference policy. Beyond a certain budget, over-optimization (or reward hacking) can arise: because we optimize against a proxy reward model (distinct from true rewards), performance can plateau or degrade. Naturally, the proxy reward's accuracy depends on how much preference data (often in the form of pairwise comparisons) was used to train it. However, existing research does not cleanly identify how performance jointly scales with the amount of training data and the divergence budget. Our main contribution is to provide an empirically accurate and theoretically grounded scaling law in such context. Performance roughly scales as $Θ(\sqrt{\min\{\log(M),K\}})$, where $M$ is the number of comparisons in training data and $K$ is the policy's divergence budget. We develop an information-theoretic model to establish this upper bound and prove it is tightly achievable through a constructive procedure. Informed by this, we conduct extensive empirical evaluations using a real-world annotation setup, whereby a large 70B gold reward model generates feedback data and proxy reward models are trained from less capable models (0.6B to 4B). Our scaling law provides an excellent fit (R2 from 97\% to 99\%), outperforms alternative specifications, and remains robust across model sizes, noise, and optimization procedures (best-of-$N$ or policy tilting). Our evidence suggests that reward optimization is analogous to a surprisingly simple selection task: choosing from a sequence of IID Gaussian random variables using noisy preference feedback.

</details>


### [182] [Does This Action Still Explain the Task? Reverse Scoring for Diffusion Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38536)
*Jiacheng Qiu,Christopher E. Mower,Jan Peters,Haitham Bou-Ammar,Matthieu Zimmer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文诊断了扩散式大语言模型（dLLM）智能体陷入"重试循环"的机制，并提出一种无需训练的补救方法 Reflect Reverse。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: dLLM 通过并行解码有望打破自回归智能体的串行延迟瓶颈，但近期评测显示这种效率优势无法转化为具身智能体能力：dLLM 智能体会反复陷入重试循环，在动作早已失败后仍不断重新发出同一动作。作者希望从机制上解释该失败并给出无需训练的解决方案。

Method: 作者将重试循环追溯到掩码解码的自适应性：采样器优先提交最自信的位置、推迟不确定位置，而在失败状态下上下文已为被推迟的决策提供了高置信度的填充——即失败动作本身，于是重试在未面对失败反馈时就被提交。作者将由此产生的动作分布扭曲建模为一种"任务盲（task-blind）"腐蚀：上下文显著的动作（如刚执行的动作）获得被放大的概率，放大因子依赖状态与动作但不依赖任务。基于该模型，作者分析了一个不变性命题：任务盲因子会从反向条件分布（给定状态与候选动作时任务的可能性）中恰好抵消，该分布与理想无腐蚀模型的任务后验一致。掩码 dLLM 天然可通过掩蔽任务 token 并去噪来评估反向条件（不同于自回归模型），代价是每个候选需要若干次并行前向。作者据此实例化为 Reflect Reverse 规则。

Result: 在四个多轮具身基准上，Reflect Reverse 相比前向打分基线提升了任务成功率与推进率。

Conclusion: 重试循环源于掩码解码的自适应性与任务盲的上下文显著性腐蚀，而该扭曲因子在反向条件中可精确抵消，因此通过掩码任务 token 并去噪评估反向条件（Reflect Reverse）是一种无需训练、有效缓解重试循环的方法，并验证了 dLLM 在具身智能体中的潜力。

Abstract: Diffusion-based large language models (dLLMs) promise to break the sequential latency bottleneck of autoregressive agents through parallel decoding, but recent evaluations show this efficiency does not transfer to embodied agentic competence: dLLM-backed agents repeatedly fall into retry loops, re-issuing an action long after it has failed. We give a mechanistic account of this failure and a training-free remedy. We trace the retry loop to the adaptivity of masked decoding: the sampler commits the positions it is most confident about and defers the uncertain ones, and at a failure state the context already offers a confident fill for the deferred decision, i.e. the failed action itself, so the retry is committed without the failure feedback ever being confronted. We model the resulting distortion of the action distribution as a task-blind corruption: contextually salient actions (e.g., the action just taken) receive inflated probability by a factor that depends on the state and the action but not on the task. Under this model, we analyse an invariance proposition: the task-blind factor cancels exactly from the reverse conditional, i.e. the likelihood of the task given the state and a candidate action, which coincides with the task posterior of an idealized uncorrupted model. Masked dLLMs evaluate the reverse conditional natively, unlike autoregressive models, by masking the task tokens and denoising, at the cost of a few parallel passes per candidate. We instantiate the rule as Reflect Reverse and evaluate it on four multi-turn embodied benchmarks, where it improves task success and progression rates over forward-scoring baselines.

</details>


### [183] [ReLaG: A Scalable Framework Generalizing Random Splits to Data with Latent Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38538)
*Anthony Lavertu,Jacob Cote,Sophie Gobeil,Jacques Corbeil,Isabeau Premont-Schwarz,Pascal Germain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ReLaG框架，通过层次隐变量建模样本相关性、结合近邻图与社区检测推断相关样本组，从而生成独立的训练-测试划分；在分子与蛋白质数据集上兼具关系感知方法的效果与更优的可扩展性，并支持自适应划分分辨率与数据规模估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当数据集包含相关样本（如生化研究常见情形）时，随机划分会产生非独立的训练-测试子集，导致泛化估计过于乐观。需要一种能识别样本相关性并生成独立划分的方法。

Method: ReLaG：模态无关框架。通过层次隐变量过程建模样本相关性，利用近邻图与社区检测推断相关样本组以构造独立训练-测试划分；另引入无标签过程，根据生产数据自适应调整划分分辨率；推断的组还可提供有效数据集大小的廉价估计。

Result: 在分子与蛋白质数据集上，ReLaG 达到与现有关系感知方法相当的性能，同时可扩展性显著更好，支持此前不切实际的大规模数据集划分。

Conclusion: ReLaG 提供了一种可扩展、模态无关的关系感知数据划分方案，使评估更贴近部署场景，并支持多样性感知的数据集扩展。代码开源，可通过 pip install relag 安装。

Abstract: Random splitting can yield non-independent train--test subsets when a dataset contains related samples, as is common in certain applications such as biochemical studies. This leads to overly optimistic generalization estimates. Here, we introduce ReLaG, a modality-agnostic framework that models sample relatedness through a hierarchical latent-variable process and infers groups of related samples using proximity graphs and community detection to produce independent train--test subsets. Across molecular and protein datasets, ReLaG matches existing relation-aware methods while scaling substantially better, enabling splits at previously impractical dataset sizes. We further introduce a label-free procedure that adapts the splitting resolution to production data, aligning evaluation with the intended deployment setting. ReLaG's inferred groups provide a cheap estimate of effective dataset size, enabling diversity-aware dataset scaling. ReLaG is open source and can be installed with pip install relag.

</details>


### [184] [When a Flatness Proxy Is Not a Function: Robustness Certificates and Training Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38540)
*Vicente Opazo,Jose Calatayud-Mateu,Cristobal Rojas,Cristian Buc Calderon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 曲率上界的有效性不足以支撑其被反演为鲁棒性证书或被微分为内在训练干预；该文以最后一层相对平坦度代理为例，证明证书会低估损失增幅超210倍，且softmax共同行偏移可使其无界，需通过行中心化修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中常把满足曲率上界的最后一层相对平坦度代理直接反演为鲁棒性证书，或对其微分以构造内在训练干预，但"上界有效"并不自动保证这两种用途成立；作者希望厘清这一逻辑跳跃。

Method: 以最后一层相对平坦度代理为对象，在两类场景下做理论分析：一是有限全局经验风险最小点处的点式一阶损失项与证书低估；二是共同行softmax偏移下的预测不变性、精确收缩性与代理无界性，并比较参考类选择（原始表示 vs 中心化表示）与标量重调参的对齐能力；辅以45组配对单步测试（算法模型与图像模型）及CIFAR-10长时程实验。

Result: 经验风险平稳性不能消除点式一阶损失项，在有限全局经验风险最小点保留的证书表达式将损失增幅低估逾210倍；给出全局有效且规范不变的特征空间修复。共同行softmax偏移保持预测与精确收缩却使代理无界，标准参考类选择平均令其翻倍；固定特征且类别数≥3时标量重调参一般无法对齐诱导的概率更新；行中心化给出轨道最小化上界，并在此对称性下恢复取值与全模型梯度不变性。45组配对单步测试中放大偏移区分了原始正则化与商正则化预测器；CIFAR-10长时程实验显示泛化被显著且可逆地抑制，而记忆后选择性延迟的证据较不一致。

Conclusion: 曲率上界意义下的有效性本身既不能证明其可反演为鲁棒性证书，也不能证明其可微分为内在训练干预；使用时需额外论证，并建议采用行中心化等规范不变修复。

Abstract: A valid curvature upper bound need not justify either a robustness certificate or an intervention on an intrinsic predictor property. We demonstrate this distinction for a last-layer relative-flatness proxy used in both settings. First, empirical-risk stationarity does not eliminate pointwise first-order loss terms: at a finite global empirical-risk minimum, the retained certificate expression underestimates a loss increase by over $210\times$. We derive a globally valid, gauge-invariant feature-space repair. Second, common-row softmax shifts preserve predictions and the exact contraction while making the proxy unbounded. Even standard reference-class choices double it on average relative to the centered representation. For a single fixed-feature example with at least three classes, scalar retuning generically cannot align the induced probability updates. Row centering gives the orbit-minimized bound and restores value and full-model gradient invariance under this symmetry. Across 45 paired one-step tests on algorithmic and image models, amplified shifts separate raw-regularized predictors while quotient-regularized predictors remain aligned. Long-horizon CIFAR-10 experiments show substantial, reversible suppression of generalization, while evidence for selective delay after memorization is less consistent. Together, these results show that validity as a curvature upper bound does not by itself justify either inversion into a robustness certificate or differentiation into an intrinsic training intervention.

</details>


### [185] [Towards Universal Wasserstein Barycenters through Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38547)
*Eduardo Fernandes Montesuma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Universal Barycenter Approximation问题，即一次性近似Wasserstein单纯形上的整族重心，并给出流匹配模型BaryFM，可在训练后通过ODE采样任意权重的重心。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Wasserstein重心方法多针对固定权重向量，近似整个Wasserstein单纯形（所有权重下的重心族）仍研究不足，需要一种能泛化到任意权重的通用重心近似方案。

Method: 提出BaryFM，一种流匹配（flow matching）模型，将边缘测度输运到Wasserstein单纯形中的任意重心；训练完成后，网络可通过求解常微分方程（ODE）从Wasserstein单纯形中的测度采样。

Result: 在4个下游任务上验证：域适应、泛化、贝叶斯后验聚合与算法公平性。在10个域适应基准上，BaryFM在15种竞争方法中取得最佳平均排名，与专用（非通用）求解器相当或更优。

Conclusion: BaryFM实现了对整个Wasserstein重心族的通用近似，兼顾通用性与竞争力，在多发下游任务上验证有效。

Abstract: Defining a weighted mean over probability measures under probability metrics is a central tool in probabilistic machine learning. Under the Wasserstein metric, these are called \emph{Wasserstein barycenters}. While most approaches compute barycenters for a fixed weight vector, approximating the whole family of barycenters over the simplex, which we call the \emph{Wasserstein simplex}, remains underexplored. We refer to this problem as \emph{Universal Barycenter Approximation}, and propose \texttt{BaryFM}, a flow matching model transporting the marginal measures into any barycenter in the Wasserstein simplex. Once trained, the network can draw samples from measures in the Wasserstein simplex through an ordinary differential equation. We validate our method on 4 downstream tasks: domain adaptation, generalization, Bayesian posterior aggregation and algorithmic fairness. \texttt{BaryFM} achieves the best average rank among 15 competing methods across 10 domain adaptation benchmarks, matching or surpassing non-universal solvers.

</details>


### [186] [The Advantages of Fresh Sketching for Ridge Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38565)
*Linkai Ma,Qilin Li,Petros Drineas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明在列采样的迭代岭回归中，每步重新采样（fresh sketching）比复用同一草图（reuse）具有可证明的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迭代随机求解器中一个核心设计选择是复用同一草图还是每步抽取新鲜随机性。对于列采样的（欠约束）迭代岭回归，新鲜草图是否能带来可证明优势此前仍是开放问题。

Method: 作者证明新鲜草图使误差仅沿当前残差解方向分析，而非对整Gram矩阵一致分析。该方向性视角为杠杆分数和岭杠杆分数采样给出更紧的收敛保证，并引出残差感知采样规则；通过最小化相关草图矩阵-向量乘积的方差，推导出oracle分布及其实际近似（含带较弱收敛保证的混合采样分布）。

Result: 理论与实验均支持结论：在合成与真实数据（包括Qwen2.5表示的岭探针）上收敛显著加快。

Conclusion: 新鲜草图在列采样迭代岭回归中确实可证明优于草图复用，方向性残差分析及残差感知采样规则是其关键。

Abstract: Over the past 25 years, sketching and sampling have become widely used tools for accelerating large-scale regression. In iterative randomized solvers, a basic design choice is whether to $\textit{reuse}$ the same sketch or draw $\textit{fresh}$ randomness at every step. For (under-constrained) iterative ridge regression with column sampling, whether fresh sketches offer provable advantages has remained open: $\textit{We show that they do.}$ Fresh sketching lets us analyze error only along the current residual solution, rather than uniformly over the entire Gram matrix. This directional view yields sharper convergence guarantees for leverage score and ridge leverage score sampling and, more importantly, leads to residual-aware sampling rules. By minimizing the variance of the relevant sketched matrix-vector product, we derive an oracle distribution and practical approximations to the oracle distribution, including a mixture sampling distribution with (somewhat weaker) convergence guarantees. Experiments on synthetic and real data, including ridge probes on Qwen2.5 representations, support our theory, showing substantially faster convergence.

</details>


### [187] [FlexRouter: Learning Complementary Model Sets for Flexible LLM Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38585)
*Wang Wei,Harry Yang,Tiankai Yang,Samyadeep Basu,Hongjie Chen,Andy Zhao,Franck Dernoncourt,Ryan A. Rossi,Hoda Eldardiry*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 FlexRouter，一种基于行列式点过程（DPP）的 LLM 路由框架，通过显式建模模型互补性并优化答案覆盖率，实现无需预设预算的自适应子集选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 路由方法对模型独立打分并选取 top-k，忽略了模型之间的相关性，且强制固定计算预算。这导致路由器常选出共享失效模式的冗余模型，降低了至少一个模型给出正确答案的概率。因此需要一种能显式建模模型互补性、并灵活控制推理成本的路由方案。

Method: 1) 将路由建模为面向覆盖率的子集选择问题，优化"答案覆盖率"，即至少一个被选模型给出正确回答的概率。2) 使用行列式点过程（DPP）建模路由策略，DPP 天然同时刻画模型能力与冗余度。3) 为在无真实目标子集的情况下直接优化覆盖率，提出基于失效集边缘化的训练目标。4) 推理时采用基于边际对数行列式增益的贪心策略，自适应确定子集大小，无需预设预算。

Result: 在 RouterEval 大规模基准上的大量实验表明，FlexRouter 在域内与域外任务上均比强基线取得更高的覆盖率与更低的冗余度，同时保持灵活的推理成本。

Conclusion: FlexRouter 通过 DPP 建模模型互补性并直接优化覆盖率，有效解决了独立打分路由带来的冗余与固定预算问题，在覆盖率和冗余度上均优于现有方法，且支持灵活自适应的推理开销。

Abstract: Existing Large Language Model (LLM) routing methods score LLMs independently to select top-$k$ models. However, this ignores model correlations and enforces a rigid computational budget. Consequently, routers often select redundant models that share failure modes, limiting the overall probability of success. To address this, we propose FlexRouter, a routing framework that explicitly models model complementarity. FlexRouter optimizes for \textit{answer coverage}, maximizing the probability that at least one selected model yields a correct response. This objective aligns with practical inference pipelines where multiple candidate outputs are generated and a downstream verifier or user selects the final one. We formulate routing as a coverage-oriented subset selection problem and model the routing policy using Determinantal Point Processes (DPPs), which naturally capture both model competence and redundancy. To directly optimize coverage without requiring a ground-truth target subset, we introduce a training objective based on marginalizing over failure sets. During inference, we employ a greedy strategy based on marginal log-determinant gains, enabling the router to adaptively determine subset sizes without a predefined budget. Extensive experiments on the large-scale RouterEval benchmark demonstrate that our proposed FlexRouter achieves higher coverage with lower redundancy across both in-domain and out-of-domain tasks than strong baselines while maintaining flexible inference cost.

</details>


### [188] [NeurDuo-EEG: A Long-Sequence EEG Foundation Model with Persistent State and Explicit Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38587)
*Yifan Wang,Haiping Liu,Yang Cui,Wenhao Cai,Shuhang Li,Xiaoyang Huang,Xianyang Liu,Jingyu Sun,Yizheng Sun,Cunhang Fan,Tianming Du,Jiancheng Yang,Zhenhong Li,Yunhao Zhang,Hongpeng Zhou,Jingyuan Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出因果EEG基础模型NeurDuo-EEG，通过通道分辨的持久记忆与多时间尺度记忆管理，实现长时序连续脑电建模，在5个下游任务中4项最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EEG连续记录达数小时，相关动态跨越毫秒至小时量级；但多数EEG基础模型对固定窗口独立处理，难以捕获长时序动态信息，状态空间架构虽支持持续循环处理，长程信息仍被隐式压缩在循环状态中。

Method: 构建因果EEG基础模型NeurDuo-EEG，引入通道分辨的持久记忆，采用带学习式巩固与选择性检索的多时间尺度记忆管理，在固定规模状态下持久建模连续EEG；使用离散频谱码的多通道自回归预测，在17个公开数据集共3955小时EEG上预训练。

Result: 在3个短窗口与2个长序列下游任务中，5个基准的4项取得最优，包括全部3个短窗口任务与癫痫检测（AUC-PR由0.285提升至0.471）；睡眠分期保持竞争力；支持高效流式推理，历史增长至1小时时每块延迟近似恒定；Small变体骨干仅4.7M参数。

Conclusion: 持久、多时间尺度建模对长序列与短窗口EEG分析均具价值。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) is recorded continuously over hours, with relevant dynamics spanning timescales from milliseconds to hours. Most EEG foundation models nevertheless process fixed windows independently, limiting their ability to capture information encoded in long-timescale dynamics. State-space architectures enable persistent recurrent processing, but long-range information remains implicitly compressed in recurrent states. We present NeurDuo-EEG, a causal EEG foundation model with channel-resolved persistent memory. NeurDuo-EEG introduces multi-timescale memory management with learned consolidation and selective retrieval, enabling persistent modelling of continuous EEG with fixed-size state. It is pre-trained on 3,955 hours of EEG from 17 public datasets using multichannel autoregressive prediction of discrete spectral codes. Across three short-window and two long-sequence downstream tasks, NeurDuo-EEG achieves the best performance on four of five benchmarks, including all three short-window tasks and seizure detection, where AUC-PR improves from $0.285$ to $0.471$ over the strongest non-NeurDuo baseline. NeurDuo-EEG also remains competitive on sleep staging and supports efficient streaming inference, with nearly constant per-chunk latency as the available history grows to one hour. Notably, the Small variant achieves this with only 4.7M backbone parameters. These results demonstrate the value of persistent, multi-timescale modelling for both long-sequence and short-window EEG analysis. Our code is available at https://github.com/YifaNNW/NeurDuo-EEG.

</details>


### [189] [Reinforcement Learning with Complex (valued) Memories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38598)
*Sathya Kamesh Bhethanabhotla,Efstratios Gavves,André Biedenkapp*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将酉循环网络（uRNN）作为PPO循环架构的替代方案，利用复数隐状态与保范酉动力学增强长程记忆，并提出相位感知策略测量方法，在多个部分可观测任务上取得2-3倍奖励增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 部分可观测环境下，智能体需压缩时序信息并维持记忆以做出有效决策。尽管已有门控循环、注意力机制与基于模型的RL等方法，如何有效表征并捕获长期依赖仍是活跃难题。

Method: 重新审视uRNN（Arjovsky等2016；Jing等2017），其将循环与隐状态表达于复数空间，保范酉动力学利于信息在长序列中传播。提出三种uRNN版本，直接替换循环PPO架构；并类比量子态测量，探索为相位感知策略保留复数隐状态的相位信息。

Result: 在rocksample、Craftax等记忆可改进任务及连续控制任务上，方法显著优于基线，奖励最高达基线的2-3倍。

Conclusion: 简单循环结构与复数相位带来的额外自由度可显著提升记忆能力，为RL表征学习与部分可观测问题开辟了新方向。代码已开源。

Abstract: Partially observable environments pose a fundamental challenge in deep reinforcement learning, requiring agents to compress temporal information from observations and maintain a memory to make effective decisions. While there exist many approaches ranging from gated recurrence to attention mechanisms and model-based RL, the search for effective representational techniques that can capture long-term dependencies remains an active area of research. In this work we revisit Unitary recurrent networks (uRNNs) [Arjovsky et al., 2016, Jing et al., 2017], that demonstrated superior gradient flow and associative recall, expressing the recurrence and the hidden state in a complex vector space. Their norm preserving unitary dynamics enable information propagation through long sequences. To this end, we propose three different versions of uRNNs as drop-in replacements for recurrent PPO architectures, and demonstrate that the simple recurrence and the added degree of freedom from the phase of the complex representations enable significant gains over baselines on several memory-improvable tasks, including continuous control. We further explore how to preserve the phase information of the complex hidden state for a phase-aware policy by drawing a parallel to how quantum states are measured. With our methods reaching up to 2-3 $\times$ the reward in environments like rocksample and Craftax compared to the baselines, this work points towards an exciting new direction of representations for RL and the problem of partial observability. Code is available at: https://github.com/Sathya98/qurl

</details>


### [190] [JARQ: Joint Alternating Refinement for Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38599)
*Xinyu Wang,Sicheng Lyu,Xiao-Wen Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 JARQ，一种免反向传播的后训练量化精修方法，通过联合最小二乘拟合组缩放与有界 Babai 提议，在不改变宿主量化器位宽、分组、零点与推理成本的前提下提升大语言模型量化精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分组式训练后量化器将权重量化到固定网格后不再根据生成的整数码重新拟合网格，导致精度损失。作者指出三个根因：最优网格依赖具体整数码；输入相关性使不同分组的误差相互耦合；有效的码值调整往往需要同时改变多个码。

Method: JARQ 作为插件式精修模块，从任意分组量化器出发，交替执行两步：一是对所有分组缩放因子进行联合最小二乘拟合；二是在当前网格上施加有界 Babai 提议，使一个分组内的多个码同时移动。该问题被建模为双线性盒约束混合整数最小二乘问题，求解过程免反向传播，在精确求解缩放时不会增加层级目标函数，并保持宿主的位宽、分组、零点与推理开销。

Result: 在 Llama-2、Llama-3 与 Qwen 模型上，以 RTN、GPTQ、OmniQuant、AWQ 为宿主进行评测：96 组对比中 90 组困惑度下降，三比特 RTN 困惑度最高降低 36%；24 种配置中 23 种平均多选准确率提升；同时改善 QEP、QuaRot、OJBKQ 的输出，7B 模型每个块耗时低于一分钟。

Conclusion: JARQ 是一种通用、低成本且即插即用的量化精修方案，可广泛适配主流分组量化器与模型，在几乎不增加推理成本的情况下稳定提升量化后模型性能。

Abstract: Group-wise post-training quantizers for large language models round weights onto a grid that is not refit to the resulting integer codes. We show that this leaves accuracy on the table: the best grid depends on the codes, input correlations couple the errors of different groups, and useful code changes often involve many codes at once. We propose JARQ , a plug-in refinement that starts from any group-wise quantizer and alternates a joint least-squares fit of all group scales with bounded Babai proposals that move many codes of a group together on the current grid. The problem is a bilinear box-constrained mixed-integer least-squares problem; the solver is backpropagation-free, does not increase the layer-wise objective under exact scale solves, and keeps the host's bit width, groups, zero points, and inference cost. Across Llama-2, Llama-3, and Qwen models with RTN, GPTQ, OmniQuant, and AWQ hosts, JARQ lowers perplexity in 90 of 96 comparisons, cuts three-bit RTN perplexity by up to 36%, raises mean multiple-choice accuracy in 23 of 24 configurations, and improves QEP, QuaRot, and OJBKQ outputs, at under a minute per 7B block.

</details>


### [191] [Learning-Enabled Estimation: Tight Characterizations under Sample Selection Biases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38608)
*Vikram Kher,Jane H. Lee,Anay Mehrotra,Manolis Zampetakis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文完整刻画了样本选择偏倚下回归可识别性成立所需的最小条件，并给出首个通用估计方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 样本选择问题广泛存在于临床试验、劳动力市场、拍卖等场景，忽视选择会导致系统性偏差。自Heckman两阶段模型以来虽有许多充分条件，但"何时可回归"的完整刻画仍属空白，尤其在选择机制日益复杂的现代场景下亟待解决。

Method: 对选择过程函数形式给出可识别性的充要刻画，确立回归可识别所需的最小假设；在此基础上做自然强化，给出有限样本估计保证（显式收敛率）与oracle高效算法。

Result: 证明存在回归函数可识别、而选择过滤器本身不可识别的情形，超越"先估计选择过滤器再纠偏"的既有范式；在强化条件下建立有限样本收敛率并给出估计方法。

Conclusion: 给出选择偏倚下回归可识别性的完整刻画与首个通用估计方法，并探讨其对拍卖进入成本、劳动力市场等复杂选择机制计量场景的启示。

Abstract: When can we learn from biased samples? We study regression when outcomes are observed only after passing through selection filters that depend on both covariates and outcomes themselves, a ubiquitous challenge spanning clinical trials with patient dropout, labor markets with self-selection, and auctions with strategic entry. Ignoring such selection yields systematically biased conclusions with real-world consequences. This challenge has a long history in econometrics and statistics, starting with Heckman's seminal two-stage model and followed by numerous generalizations. While these works provide various sufficient conditions for identification, a complete characterization of when such regression is possible has remained elusive.
  In this work, we provide a characterization for when regression is possible in the presence of sample selection bias. Our results establish the minimal assumptions required on the functional forms of selection processes under which regression remains possible, which are particularly relevant in modern settings where selection mechanisms are increasingly complex and opaque. As a corollary of our characterization, we show that there are settings where the regression function can be identified even when the selection filter itself cannot. This observation already goes beyond the ``estimate selection filter, then debias regression'' paradigm that is followed by virtually all existing approaches. Under natural strengthenings of our identification conditions, we also establish finite-sample estimation guarantees with explicit convergence rates and provide oracle-efficient algorithms. This yields the first general-purpose estimation method for this broad class of selection problems. Finally, we explore the implications of our results for several well-studied econometric settings with complex selection mechanisms such as auctions with entry costs and labor markets.

</details>


### [192] [Differentiable Structure Learning for Cyclic Linear Gaussian Models with Latent Confounders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38618)
*Sadegh Khorasani,Ali Najar,Saber Salehkaleybar,Negar Kiyavash*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究在线性高斯结构因果模型、允许有向环且存在未知数量外生潜变量（有上界）情形下的因果结构学习。作者推导了观测变量的协方差，提出“边缘准等价”刻画不同因果模型共享满维观测分布集合的条件；将结构学习建模为带对数尺度复杂度惩罚的高斯负对数似然最小化，证明全局分数最小解的相合性；用Bernoulli门连续概率化边与潜变量，得到闭式可微惩罚目标，并证明其与离散目标的全局下确界一致；实验表明恢复误差低于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统因果结构学习多假设无环（DAG）且无潜混杂，而现实数据常存在反馈环与未观测混杂。作者希望在允许有向环和未知数量有界潜混杂的线性高斯模型下，建立可识别性与相合性的结构学习框架，并解决离散结构搜索（边与潜变量选择）不可微、优化困难的问题。

Method: 1) 在线性高斯结构因果模型中引入有向环与最多给定上界的外生潜变量，推导观测变量协方差；2) 定义“边缘准等价”，刻画模型间共享满维观测分布的条件；3) 采用带对数尺度复杂度惩罚（计数有向边与潜变量）的高斯负对数似然作为评分，证明在代数忠实性、结构最小性与模型重叠假设下全局分数最小解的相合性；4) 用Bernoulli门参数化有向边与候选潜变量的纳入，连续概率与结构系数联合优化；5) 对门变量求期望，得到闭式可微复杂度惩罚目标，并证明其全局下确界与离散结构学习目标一致。

Result: 理论上：给出观测协方差表达与边缘准等价刻画；在代数忠实性、结构最小性、模型重叠假设下，证明固定观测变量数与潜变量上界时全局评分最小解在边缘准等价意义下相合；证明Bernoulli门期望目标的全局下确界等于对应离散结构学习目标的下确界，且惩罚项具有闭式可微形式。实验上：在若干实验设置中，所提方法的结构恢复误差低于以往方法。

Conclusion: 该工作将因果结构学习推广到同时存在有向环与未知数量潜混杂的线性高斯情形，通过边缘准等价与带对数惩罚的评分建立相合性，并用Bernoulli门连续松弛实现可微优化，理论与实验均显示其有效性与更优恢复性能。

Abstract: We study causal structure learning from observational data in linear Gaussian structural causal models in the presence of directed cycles and an unknown number of exogenous latent confounders, bounded by a given maximum. We derive the covariance of the observed variables and introduce marginal quasi-equivalence, which characterizes when different causal models share a full-dimensional subset of the observational distributions they can generate. We formulate structure learning as minimization of the Gaussian negative log-likelihood with a logarithmically scaled complexity penalty that counts directed edges and latent variables. For a fixed number of observed variables and a fixed upper bound on latent variables, we establish consistency of global score minimizers up to marginal quasi-equivalence under algebraic faithfulness, structural minimality, and model-overlap assumptions. We parameterize the inclusion of directed edges and candidate latent variables using Bernoulli gates, whose continuous probabilities are optimized jointly with the structural coefficients. Averaging the penalized negative log-likelihood over these gates yields an objective with a closed-form differentiable complexity penalty. We prove that this expected objective has the same global infimum as the corresponding discrete structure-learning objective. Experimental results show that our approach achieves lower recovery error than previous methods in several experimental settings.

</details>


### [193] [Geometry-physics confounding impairs PDE learning across varying domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38623)
*Yinghao Cheng,Gengxiang Chen,Xu Liu,Qinglu Meng,Yixin Jing,Xiangguo Tang,Wenping Mou,Lihui Wang,Yingguang Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出"几何-物理混杂"是变区域PDE学习失败的统一机制，并提出显式建模几何到算子变换的去混杂框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨不同区域的PDE动力学学习对预测建模与数据驱动方程发现至关重要，但几何变化同时改变场表示与支配微分算子，使几何效应与内在物理性质混杂。

Method: 提出去混杂框架：将已知的几何到算子变换显式化，构造几何诱导的系数场及几何完备的候选库。

Result: 在五个算子学习基准上，几何诱导系数场提升预测精度与数据效率；几何完备候选库能恢复生成方程，并在两个演化区域系统中将留出PDE残差降低两个数量级以上。

Conclusion: 通过分离已知几何作用与内在物理，该框架支持变几何科学工程问题中更可靠、更数据高效的PDE学习。

Abstract: Learning partial differential equation (PDE) dynamics across varying domains is central to predictive modelling and data-driven discovery of governing equations. However, geometric variation alters both field representation and the governing differential operators, confounding geometric effects with intrinsic physical properties in the observed dynamics. This work identifies geometry-physics confounding as a unified failure mechanism for PDE learning across varying domains. In forward operator learning, this confounding increases the burden of inferring geometry-dependent operator changes from finite data, reducing data efficiency and generalisation. In equation discovery, omitting geometry-induced operators misspecifies the candidate library, leading to biased parameters, missed governing terms and spurious terms. We propose a de-confounding framework that makes the known geometry-to-operator transformation explicit. Geometry-induced coefficient fields improve prediction and data efficiency across five operator-learning benchmarks, while geometry-complete candidate libraries recover the generating equations and reduce held-out PDE residuals by more than two orders of magnitude in both evolving-domain systems. By separating known geometric action from intrinsic physics, the proposed framework supports more reliable and data-efficient PDE learning across scientific and engineering problems with varying geometries.

</details>


### [194] [Interpretable but Fragile? Robustness of Concept Bottlenecks under Geometric-Semantic Perturbations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38625)
*Hanwei Zhang,Tianma Hu,Gaojie Jin,Xu Cheng,Ronghui Mu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出基于生成器的评估框架，在统一设定下区分连续几何扰动与离散语义干预两种扰动机制，实证与可证明地比较标准分类器与概念瓶颈模型（CBM）的鲁棒性，从而调和既有矛盾结论，并揭示可解释性与鲁棒性之间依赖于扰动机制和任务结构的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关于CBM是否提升鲁棒性，现有研究结论混杂甚至相互矛盾。作者认为这些分歧并非源于对CBM本身的根本性分歧，而是因为混淆了不同的鲁棒性定义与扰动机制。因此需要一套可控、可分解的评估框架来厘清在何种意义、何种条件下概念瓶颈会影响鲁棒性。

Method: 提出基于生成器的评估框架，支持标准分类器与CBM在两类扰动下的受控对比：（1）隐空间中的连续几何扰动；（2）概念空间中的离散语义干预。鲁棒性评估同时采用经验方式（预测级与概念级敏感性指标）与可证明方式（隐空间和概念空间中的随机平滑）。进一步分析类别语义相似度、概念词表规模等任务条件的影响。

Result: 框架成功调和了以往相互冲突的发现，明确了概念瓶颈在何时、以何种意义提升或不提升鲁棒性。结果表明可解释性并不天然带来鲁棒性，概念瓶颈改变的是敏感性出现的位置与方式。鲁棒性表现关键取决于扰动机制与任务结构（如类别语义相似度、概念词表大小）。

Conclusion: 可解释性与鲁棒性是两个不同的目标：可解释的中间表示并不会一致地提升鲁棒性，而是将敏感性在不同扰动空间与模型族之间重新分配，形成依赖扰动机制与任务结构的、细致的可解释性—鲁棒性权衡。

Abstract: Concept Bottleneck Models (CBMs) are designed to provide interpretable intermediate representations, yet how such bottlenecks affect robustness remains unclear, with existing studies reporting mixed and sometimes contradictory findings. We argue that these discrepancies arise from conflating different robustness notions and perturbation regimes, rather than from fundamental disagreements about CBMs themselves. To disentangle these factors, we introduce a generator-based evaluation framework that enables controlled comparisons between standard classifiers and CBMs under two distinct perturbation types: continuous geometric perturbations in latent space and discrete semantic interventions in concept space. Within this framework, we evaluate robustness both empirically, via prediction and concept-level sensitivity metrics, and certifiably, using randomized smoothing in latent and concept spaces. Across experiments, we reconcile previously conflicting findings by clarifying when, and in what sense, concept bottlenecks do or do not improve robustness. By further analyzing robustness under varying task conditions, including class semantic similarity and concept vocabulary size, we show that interpretability does not inherently confer robustness. Instead, concept bottlenecks shift where and how sensitivity manifests, revealing a nuanced interpretability robustness trade off that depends critically on the perturbation regime and task structure. Together, our results show that interpretability and robustness are distinct objectives: interpretable intermediate representations do not uniformly improve robustness, but instead redistribute sensitivity across perturbation spaces and model families.

</details>


### [195] [Proper Scoring Rule-based Diffusion for Probabilistic Weather Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38632)
*Joonhyeong Park,Giung Nam,Hyungi Lee,Kyunghyun Cho,Byoungwoo Park,Juho Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出在基于CRPS训练的随机天气预报模型中引入辅助条件去噪任务，通过单个随机预测器最小化跨噪声水平下的适当评分规则来学习条件分布，在推理时仍可单次前向生成集合成员，显著提升长预报时段的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有概率天气预报模型仅依赖预报背景信息用CRPS训练随机预测器，在长预报时段不确定性高时，单从背景难以有效学习预测分布，导致预测模糊。

Method: 引入辅助条件去噪任务：从背景及其加噪版本预测同一未来状态，提供部分未来信息以降低预测歧义。基于分布扩散模型，用单个随机预测器跨噪声水平最小化适当评分规则来学习这些任务的条件分布，推理时在完全加噪端点单次前向生成集合成员。标准CRPS训练是仅端点特例。

Result: 受控实验表明辅助任务在不同架构上改善一步预报，长预报时段增益更大；在高维全球天气预报中（从头训练和微调）均获增益，并改进了校准，对分布偏移下的泛化有潜在益处。

Conclusion: 该框架将现有CRPS预报器扩展为仅需额外条件输入的形式，通过辅助去噪任务有效提升长时段概率天气预报性能、校准与泛化能力。

Abstract: Recent probabilistic weather forecasters train stochastic predictors with the continuous ranked probability score (CRPS) to generate each ensemble member in a single forward pass. These models learn the predictive distribution from the forecast context alone, which becomes difficult at longer forecast horizons where uncertainty is high. To learn the predictive distribution more effectively, we introduce auxiliary conditional denoising tasks that predict the same future state from the context and its corrupted version, which provides partial future information that can reduce prediction ambiguity. Building on distributional diffusion models, we learn the conditional distributions of these tasks with a single stochastic predictor by minimizing a proper scoring rule across noise levels. At inference, the predictor can still generate each ensemble member in a single forward pass at the fully corrupted endpoint. Standard CRPS training is recovered as the endpoint-only special case of our formulation, so our framework extends existing CRPS-based forecasters with only additional conditioning inputs. Controlled experiments show that the auxiliary tasks improve one-step forecasting across architectures, with larger gains at longer forecast horizons. The gains extend to high-dimensional global weather forecasting under both training from scratch and fine-tuning, along with improved calibration and potential benefits for generalization under distribution shift.

</details>


### [196] [Alignment via Training Against Probes Without Losing Monitorability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38645)
*Lena Libon,Alexander Panfilov,Ben Rank,Xin Chen,Jonas Geiping,Maksym Andriushchenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"探针引导微调"（probe-guided fine-tuning），用检测模型激活中不良属性的探针作为训练信号，直接塑造模型的内部表征。实验表明，持续更新的探针能显著降低有害性、提升诚实性并保持实用性，在安全-效用权衡上优于DPO和推理时引导，且对越狱和消融攻击更鲁棒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐方法通常基于模型的外部输出（示范、偏好数据或奖励信号）进行训练，这类目标奖励的是"看起来对齐"的响应。能力更强的模型可能学会在不内化目标行为的情况下满足这些目标（如训练中假装顺从）。当目标定义在模型内部而非输出上时，这种表面顺从会更难实现，因此作者转向研究基于内部表征的对齐方式。

Method: 提出探针引导微调：使用检测模型激活中不良属性的探针作为直接训练信号。评估了线性与非线性探针、以及每层不同探针数量，在无害性和诚实性两个对齐目标下的效果。关键设计对比是：训练中不更新的探针 vs. 持续更新的探针。

Result: 使用不更新的探针作为训练目标容易被利用；持续更新的探针能大幅降低有害性并提升诚实性，同时保持实用性。探针引导微调在安全-效用权衡上优于DPO和推理时引导，且对越狱攻击和消融（abliteration）攻击的鲁棒性明显更强。此外，微调后相关概念仍保持线性编码，说明该方法并未丧失对模型的可监督性。

Conclusion: 基于探针训练提供了一种塑造模型"表征内容"而非仅仅"输出内容"的方法。随着模型越来越善于让输出显得对齐，这种从内部表征入手的对齐路径可能愈发重要。

Abstract: Models are usually aligned based on their observed outputs, using demonstrations, preference data, or reward signals. These objectives reward responses that look aligned. More capable models may learn to satisfy them without internalizing the intended behavior, for example by faking compliance during training. Such superficial compliance could be harder when the objective is defined on model internals rather than outputs. Therefore, we study probe-guided fine-tuning, using probes that detect undesired properties in model activations as a direct training signal. We evaluate linear and non-linear probes with different numbers of probes per layer across two alignment objectives: harmlessness and honesty. We find that training against probes that do not update during training is an easily exploitable objective, while continuously updated probes substantially reduce harmfulness and improve honesty while preserving utility. Probe-guided fine-tuning achieves better safety-utility trade-offs than DPO and inference-time steering, while being substantially more robust against jailbreak and abliteration attacks. Moreover, the concepts stay linearly encoded after fine-tuning, meaning oversight is not lost by our method. Training against probes thus offers a way to shape what models represent rather than only what they output, which may become increasingly important as models get better at making their outputs look aligned.

</details>


### [197] [Uncertainty-Normalized Margins for Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38647)
*Sadegh Khorasani,Petrus Mikkola,Matthias Grossglauser*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出UNM-DPO，通过异方差Bradley-Terry模型引入强度相关margin与可学习的prompt尺度，并扩展出长度归一化的ULNM-DPO-WR，在多个评测上优于DPO与SimPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DPO用单一噪声尺度的Bradley-Terry模型建模二元偏好，未显式考虑偏好强度与由人类反馈带来的prompt相关不确定性。

Method: 提出UNM-DPO，结合强度相关margin与学习到的prompt尺度；基于异方差Bradley-Terry模型设计两种目标：Advantage-only(AO)先用prompt尺度归一化隐式奖励差再减margin，whole-residual(WR)先减margin再除以尺度；对WR给出已知margin下prompt尺度可识别的充要条件并给出尺度学习流程；进一步提出ULNM-DPO-WR，用响应长度归一化每个响应的隐式奖励。

Result: 在HelpSteer2与HelpSteer3上以Skywork奖励模型为judge评测；Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct上ULNM-DPO-WR对matched DPO的tie-adjusted胜率为68.00%与65.31%，平均奖励更高且回复更短。在AlpacaEval上以GPT-4.1为judge、GPT-4-Turbo为参考，该8B策略长度控制胜率21.62%，高于DPO的16.39%与SimPO的15.30%。

Conclusion: 结合偏好强度margin、可学习prompt尺度与长度归一化可有效提升策略优化效果。

Abstract: Direct preference optimization (DPO) models binary preferences through a Bradley-Terry model with a common noise scale, without explicitly accounting for preference strength or prompt-dependent uncertainty from human feedback. We introduce uncertainty-normalized margin DPO (UNM-DPO), which combines strength-dependent margins with a learned prompt scale. Motivated by a heteroskedastic Bradley-Terry model, we develop two training objectives. Both compare the implicit rewards of preferred and rejected responses, derived from response log-probability ratios to a reference policy. Advantage-only (AO) divides this reward difference by the prompt scale before subtracting the margin; whole-residual (WR) subtracts the margin before dividing by the scale. For the WR comparison model, we establish a necessary and sufficient condition under which known margins make the prompt scale identifiable. We introduce a practical procedure for learning the scale. Building on WR, we introduce ULNM-DPO-WR, which normalizes each response's implicit reward by its length. We evaluate our methods against DPO and related baselines on HelpSteer2 and HelpSteer3, using the Skywork reward model as a judge. With Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, ULNM-DPO-WR achieves tie-adjusted win rates against matched DPO of 68.00% and 65.31% on evaluation panels, with higher mean rewards and shorter responses on average. On AlpacaEval with a GPT-4.1 judge and GPT-4-Turbo reference answers, the same 8B policy achieves a length-controlled win rate of 21.62%, compared with 16.39% for DPO and 15.30% for SimPO. These results demonstrate the potential of combining preference-strength margins, learned prompt scales, and length normalization for policy optimization.

</details>


### [198] [Understanding Off- vs On-Policy Distillation: A Tale of Distinct Training Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38666)
*Qiwei Di,Xuheng Li,Kaixuan Ji,Chenggong Zhang,Heyang Zhao,Quanquan Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究多教师序列化蒸馏中前向/反向KL的聚合目标差异，提出离策略与在策略学习算法并给出对数遗憾界，揭示OPD优势与脆弱性的机理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在策略蒸馏（OPD）相比监督微调（SFT）能减少遗忘，但其收益与脆弱性尚未被充分理解。作者希望通过研究多教师序列化蒸馏，厘清不同KL散度目标如何影响学生模型的学习效果与稳定性。

Method: 学生最小化其与多教师之间的平均散度。前向KL产生加权算术混合目标，反向KL产生归一化加权几何聚合目标。作者分别针对离策略与在策略反馈设计学习算法，在表格设定下建立对数遗憾界，并将分析推广至函数逼近。

Result: 通过分析两种聚合目标发现：相对前向KL，反向KL在无信息反馈下能更好地保留自信专家的偏好，但对给正确回答分配极低概率的教师更敏感；其token级条件分布还揭示了对续写分布的依赖，在长时程中可能偏好错误前缀。

Conclusion: 研究为OPD的收益与脆弱性提供了统一的聚合视角与理论解释，指出KL方向选择在蒸馏中的关键权衡，并给出可扩展到函数逼近的分析框架。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) learns from teacher feedback on student-generated responses and has shown promise in reducing forgetting relative to supervised fine-tuning (SFT). However, its benefits and fragility remain incompletely understood. We study sequential distillation from multiple teachers, where the student minimizes its average divergence from the teachers. Forward Kullback--Leibler (KL) divergence yields a weighted arithmetic mixture, while reverse KL yields a normalized weighted geometric aggregate. We develop algorithms that learn these targets under off-policy and on-policy feedback, respectively, establishing logarithmic regret bounds in the tabular setting and extending the analysis to function approximation. By analyzing these aggregation targets, we identify mechanisms that help explain both the benefits and fragility of OPD. Relative to forward KL, reverse KL can better retain a confident expert's preferences under uninformative feedback, but is more sensitive to teachers that assign very low probabilities to correct responses. Its token-level conditionals also reveal a dependence on continuation distributions that can favor incorrect prefixes over long horizons.

</details>


### [199] [Provable Test-Time Scaling for Beam Search in LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38672)
*Qijia He,Yu Huang,Yuan Cheng,Yuxin Chen,Yingbin Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从理论上研究beam search的测试时计算保证，提出置信过滤束搜索（CF-Beam），将充分覆盖率依赖从二次降至近线性，并证明其优于Best-of-N等方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Beam search等基于束搜索的测试时方法在长程生成中经验上很成功，能提前剪枝无效推理路径，改善推理效率和测试时成本扩展，但其理论理解仍然有限。

Method: 研究使用模型内部对数似然做中间评分、仅在完整响应生成后用外部奖励模型评分的beam search框架；首先建立vanilla beam search的下界，然后提出修改后的置信过滤束搜索（CF-Beam）。

Result: 证明vanilla beam search至少需要Ω(C*(x)^2)个样本才能使最优响应存活，其中C*(x)为提示x的token级覆盖系数；CF-Beam在prefix competitiveness下将充分覆盖率依赖从二次降至近线性。CF-Beam的regret由稀有失败事件概率和经路径级覆盖系数缩放的奖励估计误差上界，稀有失败项随每步采样增加而消失。

Conclusion: CF-Beam通过token级覆盖系数（随horizon L多项式增长）控制主导搜索项，而Best-of-N和Best-of-Majority等序列级方法的覆盖系数随L指数增长，表明beam search具有根本优势；数值实验证实beam search在困难实例和更长推理horizon下更稳健。

Abstract: Beam-search-based test-time methods provide an effective way to improve large language model (LLM) performance on long-horizon generation by pruning invalid reasoning paths early, leading to significantly improved reasoning efficiency and more favorable test-time cost scaling. Despite strong empirical success, the theoretical understanding of beam search remains limited. In this paper, we study the test-time compute guarantee of the commonly used beam search framework that uses the model's internal log-likelihood for intermediate scoring, while relying on an external reward model only after a complete response is generated. We first establish a lower bound for vanilla beam search, showing that at least $Ω(C^\star(x)^2)$ samples are required for the optimal response to survive, where $C^\star(x)$ is the token-level coverage coefficient for prompt $x$. This motivates our modified confidence-filtered beam search (CF-Beam), which reduces the sufficient coverage dependence from quadratic to nearly linear under prefix competitiveness, for fixed horizon, gap, and target accuracy. We then show that the regret of CF-Beam is upper-bounded by the probability of rare failure events and the reward estimation error scaled by a path-level coverage coefficient, where the rare-failure term vanishes as per-step sampling increases. Our results highlight a fundamental advantage of beam search over sequence-level inference methods such as Best-of-N and Best-of-Majority. While the guarantees of these approaches typically involve coverage coefficients that grow exponentially with the horizon $L$, CF-Beam controls the dominant search-induced term through a token-level coverage coefficient that scales polynomially with $L$. Our numerical experiments further confirm that beam search is more robust on hard instances and under increasing reasoning horizons.

</details>


### [200] [In-Distribution Imagination for Model-Based Offline Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38673)
*Mintae Kim,Koushil Sreenath*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出in-distribution imagination (IDI)框架，通过在表示空间中估计轨迹支撑并自适应截断偏离离线轨迹流形的rollout，实现轨迹级rollout控制，配合轨迹正则化RL在有限数据下稳定提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于模型的离线强化学习（MBORL）通过模型生成轨迹提升样本效率，但累积模型误差会使想象轨迹偏离离线数据分布，产生不真实的合成数据并导致策略优化不稳定。现有方法多用转移级不确定性控制rollout，粒度不足。

Method: IDI在学习到的表示空间中估计轨迹支撑（trajectory support），自适应截断离开离线轨迹流形的rollout；并与trajectory-regularized RL（熵正则化RL的扩展）结合使用。

Result: 实验表明轨迹支撑对rollout失败的预测能力显著优于转移级不确定性；在有限数据设置下，IDI持续提升性能。

Conclusion: 轨迹级rollout控制对MBORL至关重要，轨迹支撑是比转移级不确定性更有效的控制信号。

Abstract: Model-based offline reinforcement learning (MBORL) improves sample efficiency through model-generated trajectories. However, accumulative model error can drive imagined trajectories outside the offline data distribution, leading to unrealistic synthetic data and unstable policy optimization. Many existing methods primarily control rollouts using transition-level uncertainty. We propose \emph{in-distribution imagination} (IDI), a rollout control framework that estimates trajectory support in a learned representation space and adaptively truncates rollouts that leave the offline trajectory manifold. Combined with trajectory-regularized RL, an extension of entropy-regularized RL, IDI consistently improves performance in limited-data settings. Experiments show that trajectory support predicts rollout failure substantially better than transition-level uncertainty, highlighting the importance of trajectory-level rollout control in MBORL.

</details>


### [201] [Molecular Property Prediction under Structural Shift with Tabular Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38744)
*Jinmo Lee,Dooho Lee,Minho Jeong,Jaemin Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文研究分子性质预测中的结构泛化问题，提出MolPAIR框架，通过结合分子级与分子对上下文进行上下文学习精炼，无需任务特定参数更新，在58个基准任务中显著提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测与训练分子结构不同的化合物性质对药物发现和材料设计至关重要。表格基础模型（TFM）通过上下文学习提供了有前景的方法，但其在结构偏移下的表现及分子比较的价值尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出MolPAIR框架：先由一个全局表格基础模型基于标注分子样本预测查询分子的性质，再由第二个冻结的TFM预测查询分子与参考分子之间预测误差的差异，利用这些比较来精炼初始预测。全程不进行任务特定的参数更新。

Result: 在58个MoleculeACE和Polaris任务上，CheMeleon表示结合TabPFN-3已在多数任务上超越所有评估基线；MolPAIR在46/58个任务上进一步提升了该预测器，且在四种分子表示和三种TFM主干上均取得增益。

Conclusion: 显式的分子比较能够强化表格上下文学习在结构泛化上的表现，同时保持分子编码器和预训练模型权重固定。代码与数据集已开源。

Abstract: Predicting molecular properties for compounds that differ structurally from labeled training molecules is important for drug discovery and materials design. Tabular foundation models (TFMs) offer a promising approach through in-context learning, but their performance under structural shifts and the value of molecular comparisons in this setting remain underexplored. We study structural generalization in molecular property prediction and introduce MolPAIR (Molecular Pair-Augmented In-context Refinement), a framework that combines molecule-level and molecular-pair contexts without task-specific parameter updates. A global tabular foundation model (TFM) first predicts a query's property from labeled molecular examples. A second frozen TFM predicts differences in prediction errors between the query and labeled reference molecules, using these comparisons to refine the initial prediction. Across 58 MoleculeACE and Polaris tasks, CheMeleon representations combined with TabPFN-3 already outperform each evaluated baseline on a majority of tasks. MOLPAIR further improves this predictor on 46 of 58 tasks, with gains across four molecular representations and three TFM backbones. These results show that explicit molecular comparisons can strengthen tabular in-context learning for structural generalization while keeping the molecular encoder and pretrained model weights fixed. The code and datasets are available at https://github.com/nums-ai/MolPAIR.

</details>


### [202] [Lasting Effects of Abstract Pretraining Beyond Perplexity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38764)
*Zachary Shinnick,Hemanth Saratchandran,Damien Teney,Anton van den Hengel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在小型语言模型中，使用仅占预训练token 1%的抽象栈操作任务进行预热，可在困惑度不变的情况下显著提升多跳问答能力，且该收益对数据结构、任务类型和训练时机高度敏感。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"语言模型应从随机初始化开始预训练"的惯例，探索抽象、算法生成数据的早期预热能否为自然语言学习提供更好起点，并揭示此类预热所塑造的具体能力及其迁移机制。

Method: 采用需要组合与状态追踪能力的抽象栈操作任务作为预热数据，将其置于预训练初期（仅占1% token），随后进行自然语言预训练；通过对照实验比较栈与队列任务、顺序推理与独立事实组合/比较任务、以及混合训练与专用初始阶段、预训练后暴露等不同时机设置。

Result: 预热使MUSIQUE多跳问答提升最高3.9 F1，HOTPOTQA与2WIKIMULTIHOPQA亦有增益，而语言建模困惑度相当；显著加速更深推理链的习得。队列任务无同等收益；增益仅体现在顺序推理链，独立事实组合/比较任务无一致收益；混合抽象数据效果远逊于专用初始阶段，预训练后暴露则收益消失。该早期优势在后续数十亿token训练中持续存在。

Conclusion: 早期抽象训练能够可靠地塑造语言模型后续获得的能力，且这种迁移由数据结构的组合/状态追踪特性、任务的顺序推理性质以及训练时机共同决定。

Abstract: Language models are typically pretrained from random initialization. Recent work challenges this convention, showing that a brief warm-up on abstract, algorithmically generated data can provide a better starting point for subsequent learning of natural language. In this paper, we show that in small language models, such a warm-up improves specific capabilities that are not reflected in language-modeling perplexity. Our warm-up uses an abstract stack-manipulation task that requires compositional and state-tracking capabilities. Allocating as little as 1% of pretraining tokens to this data improves multi-hop question answering by up to 3.9 F1 points on MUSIQUE, with additional gains on HOTPOTQA and 2WIKIMULTIHOPQA despite comparable language-modeling perplexity. Controlled experiments show that the warm-up substantially accelerates the acquisition of deeper reasoning chains. We also explore what drives this transfer. First, the structure of the data matters: replacing the stack task with a queue fails to produce the same gains. Second, the gains are specific: performance improves on sequential reasoning chains, with no consistent benefit on tasks that combine or compare independent facts. Third, timing matters: mixing abstract data with natural language is far less effective than an initial dedicated phase, and exposure after pretraining completely removes the benefits. The early advantage persists through billions of subsequent language tokens. These results show that early abstract training can reliably shape the capabilities language models later acquire.

</details>


### [203] [dattri-LLM: A Unified and Efficient Library for Training Data Attribution at LLM Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38767)
*Shixuan Liu,Tongli Zhou,Junwei Deng,Pingbang Hu,Jiaqi W. Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: dattri-LLM 是一个面向大语言模型的可扩展训练数据归因（TDA）库，通过紧凑梯度表示、基于成本模型的动态梯度算子路由、无需修改训练循环的梯度捕获机制，以及可复用的梯度算子与训练期回调接口，在效率、兼容性和可扩展性上显著提升基于梯度的归因方法的实用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练数据归因旨在估计单个训练样本对模型输出的贡献。多数可扩展的 TDA 方法依赖逐样本梯度，但在 LLM 规模下，逐样本梯度的计算与使用在效率、兼容性和可扩展性三方面均面临挑战，导致这些方法难以在实际大规模训练流程中落地。

Method: 提出 dattri-LLM 库：1）效率方面，采用紧凑梯度表示，并基于成本模型对梯度算子进行动态路由；2）兼容性方面，其捕获机制可从现有调用 backward() 的训练循环中收集逐样本梯度，无需修改循环或其配置，支持 DDP、FSDP 等分布式训练，以及基于 HuggingFace Transformers、TRL 和 OLMo 构建的流水线；3）可扩展性方面，暴露可复用的梯度算子与训练期回调，用于实现各类归因方法与应用。

Result: 支持多种归因方法，包括梯度相似度、基于曲率的影响函数以及基于轨迹的方法，并支持训练期作用于梯度的应用（如在线数据选择）。在相同硬件与工作负载下，dattri-LLM 的平均吞吐量达到最快同类库的 3.2 倍，可将多种归因方法扩展至 110B 参数模型（跨 4 张 H200 GPU），并在不同模型家族与规模上提供更优的归因保真度—成本权衡。

Conclusion: dattri-LLM 通过统一的效率优化、训练循环兼容机制与可扩展接口，使基于梯度的训练数据归因在 LLM 规模下真正可行，并在吞吐量、模型规模可扩展性与保真度—成本权衡方面优于现有方案。

Abstract: Training data attribution (TDA) estimates the contribution of individual training examples to model outputs. Most scalable TDA methods rely on per-example gradients, whose computation and use at LLM scale pose challenges in efficiency, compatibility, and extensibility. We introduce dattri-LLM, a TDA library that makes gradient-based attribution more practical at scale. For efficiency, dattri-LLM uses compact gradient representations and dynamically routes gradient operations based on a cost model. For compatibility, its capture mechanism collects per-example gradients from existing training loops that call backward(), without requiring changes to the loop or its configuration. This includes distributed training with DDP and FSDP and pipelines built with HuggingFace Transformers, TRL, and OLMo. For extensibility, dattri-LLM exposes reusable gradient operations and training-time callbacks for implementing attribution methods and applications. These interfaces support a variety of attribution methods, including gradient similarity, curvature-based influence, and trajectory-based methods, as well as applications that act on gradients during training, such as online data selection. On the same hardware and workload, dattri-LLM achieves 3.2x the throughput of the fastest competing library on average, scales multiple attribution methods to 110B-parameter models across four H200 GPUs, and offers superior attribution fidelity-cost trade-offs across a range of models with different model families and scales.

</details>


### [204] [Learning Under Forgetting: Statistical Support-Selective Retention in Stochastic Training Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38768)
*Fujie Gao,Zuyue Zhang,Gang Sun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出"重复强化与持续遗忘"(RPF)动力学框架，将遗忘视为选择性保留机制而非失败模式，通过三层理论（独立特征、共享参数、小步长/范数近似）与实验验证，说明重复强化和持续遗忘共同构成可控的归纳偏置来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究表明神经网络在训练中表现出对低复杂度结构的隐式偏好（如谱偏置）、记忆动力学和类压缩效应，但缺乏关于"选择性保留"的统一动力学解释，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 构建RPF动力学最小框架，分三层递进推演：一，在独立特征模型中推导曝光选择性生存律与支撑依赖的保留边界；二，在共享参数模型中证明遗忘对协方差模态产生谱滤波，保留强支撑共享成分、抑制弱成分；三，在小步长及范数/编码近似下，揭示RPF诱导数据拟合与信息存储代价之间的隐式权衡，产生类MDL压缩。并通过标量记忆与非线性共享网络的受控实验进行验证。

Result: 实验在标量记忆和非线性共享网络上为该强化—遗忘选择机制提供了证据：联合强化与衰减干预会改变条件保留率；在匹配曝光次数下，强化时机的效应依赖于遗忘，且保留集合的组成发生变化。

Conclusion: 重复强化与持续遗忘共同提供了一种独立于神经架构与规模的可控归纳偏置来源。

Abstract: Prior work has shown that neural networks exhibit implicit biases toward low-complexity structure (e.g., spectral bias), memorization dynamics, and compression-like effects during training, but a unified dynamical account of selective retention remains incomplete. We propose Repeated Reinforcement with Persistent Forgetting (RPF) dynamics, a minimal framework in which repeated exposure reinforces patterns and structures that recur in the data, while persistent forgetting attenuates learned information. This view treats forgetting not merely as a failure mode, but as a selection mechanism. We build the theory in three successive layers. First, in an independent-feature model, we derive an exposure-selective survival law and a support-dependent retention boundary characterizing which patterns persist under forgetting. Second, in a shared-parameter model, we show that forgetting induces spectral filtering over covariance modes, preserving strongly supported shared components while suppressing weak ones. Third, under small-step and norm/coding approximations, we show how RPF dynamics induce an implicit trade-off between data fitting and the cost of stored information, yielding Minimum Description Length (MDL)-like compression. Controlled experiments provide evidence for this reinforcement--forgetting selection mechanism in scalar memories and a nonlinear shared network. Joint reinforcement and attenuation interventions shift conditional retention, while matched exposure counts reveal forgetting-dependent effects of reinforcement timing and changes in the composition of the retained set. Together, these results show that repeated reinforcement and persistent forgetting jointly provide a controllable source of inductive bias beyond neural architecture and scale.

</details>


### [205] [Distilling Diffusion Score Discrepancy for Efficient Training Data Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38776)
*Shixuan Liu,Joan Serrà,Kin Wai Cheuk,Jinju Kim,Woosung Choi,Yukara Ikemiya,Wei-Hsiang Liao,Jiaqi W. Ma,Yuki Mitsufuji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出直接基于局部得分差异（local score discrepancy）的训练数据归因方法。TID 通过 Kronecker 分解曲率实现免重训练、免逐样本梯度存储的估计；TIDE 将 TID 蒸馏为前向学生模型，在线学习复现教师排序。在 CIFAR-10、ArtBench-10、MS-COCO 的反事实评估中，TID 匹配或超越 SOTA，TIDE 在保持大部分精度的同时将每查询成本降低四到五个数量级，毫秒级完成归因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型的训练数据归因旨在识别影响某个生成样本的训练样本。现有方法存在两大局限：一是需要昂贵的逐样本梯度计算或针对特定查询的模型优化；二是多数方法归因的是代理损失（proxy loss）的变化，而非模型实际生成行为的变化。因此需要一种直接刻画生成行为变化、且无需重训练的归因方法。

Method: 1) 直接用局部得分差异度量（local score discrepancy）来定义归因，适用于任意扩散变体（DDPM、EDM、flow matching）；2) 证明该度量可在不重训练的情况下估计为一种预条件梯度相似度（preconditioned gradient similarity）；3) 实例化为 TID，利用 Kronecker 分解曲率避免随机投影和逐样本梯度存储；4) 将 TID 蒸馏为 TIDE，一个仅前向的学生模型，在线训练以从扩散模型内部激活复现教师的排序。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10、ArtBench-10 和 MS-COCO 上进行反事实评估：TID 匹配或优于现有最先进方法；TIDE 保留 TID 的大部分准确率，同时每查询成本降低四到五个数量级，可在毫秒级归因生成样本，甚至快于生成过程本身。

Conclusion: 通过将归因直接建立在局部得分差异上，可实现与扩散变体无关、无需重训练的高效训练数据归因；TID 提供高质量归因，TIDE 通过蒸馏将其加速至毫秒级，使大规模扩散模型的训练数据归因在实用性上大幅提升。

Abstract: Training data attribution for diffusion models aims to identify the training samples that influence a generated instance, but existing methods either require costly per-sample gradient computation or query-specific model optimization. Moreover, most methods attribute changes in a proxy loss rather than changes in the actual model's generative behavior. We address these limitations by formulating attribution directly with a local score discrepancy measure, which applies to any diffusion variant (including DDPM, EDM, and flow matching), and by showing that such measure can be estimated without retraining, as a preconditioned gradient similarity. We instantiate this estimator as Training-data Influence via score Discrepancy (TID), which uses Kronecker-factored curvature to avoid random projections and per-sample gradient storage. We then distill TID into TIDE, a forward-only student trained online to reproduce the teacher's rankings from the diffusion model's internal activations. Under counterfactual evaluation on CIFAR-10, ArtBench-10, and MS-COCO, TID matches or outperforms state-of-the-art approaches, while TIDE retains most of TID's accuracy at four to five orders of magnitude lower per-query cost, attributing generated samples in milliseconds and faster than the generation itself.

</details>


### [206] [ChartDensity-Bench: Benchmarking MLLMs for Numerical Data Reconstruction under Visual Density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38781)
*Xinhe Wu,Yadong Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了ChartDensity-Bench，一个在受控视觉密度下评估MLLM从复合图表中重建结构化数值数据的基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图表理解基准主要评估问答或图表级推理，对从科学图表中重建结构化数值数据的支持有限，且MLLM在此任务上的能力尚不明确。

Method: 基于图表与源级真值数据配对构建基准，系统性地变化同时呈现的图表数量（k∈{1,3,6,9}）以控制视觉密度；并提出涵盖结构可靠性、重建完整性、可解析性和数值保真度的多维评估框架。

Result: 在五个近期MLLM上的实验显示，数值重建能力随视觉密度增加而普遍下降，且下降幅度因模型而异；图表级配对比较表明同一源图表在更密集的视觉上下文中会产生更高的重建误差。

Conclusion: 视觉密度是MLLM图表数据重建中一个重要且此前未被充分探索的因素，该基准为评估此场景下的模型鲁棒性提供了系统化工具。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) offer a promising approach for recovering numerical data from scientific charts, but their ability to reconstruct chart data from visually dense figures remains poorly understood. Existing chart understanding benchmarks primarily evaluate question answering or chart-level reasoning and provide limited support for evaluating structured numerical reconstruction from scientific figures. We introduce \textbf{ChartDensity-Bench}, a benchmark for evaluating MLLMs on structured numerical data reconstruction from compound chart figures under controlled visual density. Built from charts paired with source-level ground-truth data, ChartDensity-Bench systematically varies the number of simultaneously presented charts ($k\in{1,3,6,9}$), enabling controlled evaluation of density-induced degradation. We further propose a multi-dimensional evaluation framework covering structural reliability, reconstruction completeness, parseability, and numerical fidelity. Experiments on five recent MLLMs show that numerical reconstruction generally degrades as visual density increases, while the magnitude of degradation varies substantially across models. Chart-level paired comparisons further show that the same source chart can incur higher reconstruction error when embedded in denser visual contexts. These findings highlight visual density as an important and previously underexplored factor in MLLM chart data reconstruction and provide a systematic benchmark for evaluating model robustness in this setting.

</details>


### [207] [Does a Shared Temperature Imply a Shared Angular Scale in Probabilistic Contrastive Learning?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38784)
*Ningkang Peng,Qianfeng Yu,Jingyang Mao,Xiaoqian Peng,Tingyu Lu,Peirong Ma,Yanhui Gu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究高维分布化类别表示下vMF概率对比学习中的温度/相似度尺度问题，证明ProCo所用的vMF得分保留类别依赖的主导角增益g_c=A_c/τ，并给出理论与实验验证及“纯角度”编辑方法。
